We can exploit Toeplitz matrix structure using scipy.sparse.diags. A row k, col i nonzero when 0≤k-i<n -> i∈[k-n+1,k]. Let's detail algorithms.

Data normalization: Use sum = 1.0 for internal. Score = 2*n*max(c). For final, scale arbitrary; evaluation clamps >1000; if sum=1, values ~1/n=0.00024, far from 1000. Good.

FFT helpers:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n = len(a); L = 2*n-1; size=1<<(L-1).bit_length(); A=fft(a,size); c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); return c[:L]
def score(a,c=None):
    if c is None: c=conv_self(a)
    return 2*n*np.max(c)
```
Since sum=1. But due numeric maybe max small negative? c nonnegative but FFT errors ~1e-16. Use max(0, max_real). For score, max(c) positive.

For smooth gradient:
```
def smooth_gradient(a, c, tau):
    L=len(c)
    # stable logsumexp with temperature tau: q_k = exp((c_k-cmax)/tau) normalized. If tau very small, underflow okay, sum nonzero.
    shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
    # clip shifted min -700
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700, 0)
    q = np.exp(shifted); q /= q.sum()
    # compute h = 2 * ∑ q_k a_{k-i}
    # Use FFT: a_rev length n, q length L. out_len = L+n-1 = 3n-2, fsize pow2
    out_len = L + n -1
    fsize = 1 << (out_len-1).bit_length()
    Q = fft(q, fsize); R=fft(a_rev, fsize); h = 2*np.real(ifft(Q*R))[n-1:2*n-1]
    return h,q
```

LP Weight Solver:
Input a (n), c (L), T. Choose active indices K = top T c (maybe sorted). We want find y≥0 sum=1 minimizing t such that (a * y) h_i ≤ t. Build B (n x T) with B[i,p] = a[K[p]-i] if valid. Objective min t. Use `scipy.optimize.linprog`.
Formulation:
Variables x = [y_0..y_{T-1}, t].
Objective c_obj = [0,...,0,1].
A_ub: n rows for B y - t ≤ 0; also -? y lower bounds? y≥0, t≥0. For linprog, A_ub shape (n, T+1): columns y = B, column t = -1. b_ub = zeros(n).
A_eq: sum y =1: [ones(T),0]. b_eq=[1].
bounds: y (0,None) T, t (0,None). But t could be negative if B y≤0? B nonnegative, y sum1, t positive. Good.
If T=1, trivial y=1.
Potential issue: B dense-ish: each column has up to n nonzeros. For T=200, 819k entries. Use sparse COO.

```

    i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)

```

Line search implementation:
```
def trial(a, dir, alpha):
    a_trial = a * np.exp(-alpha * dir)
    # if underflow, set min and renorm
    a_trial = np.where(a_trial > 0, a_trial, 0)
    s=a_trial.sum()
    if s<=0: fail
    a_trial/=s (or scale to S=1)
    c_trial=conv_self(a_trial)
    obj_trial=score(c_trial)
    return obj_trial
```
Backtracking: α = current_step. For attempt in range(12): if obj_trial <= obj_cur*(1-1e-12) accept, increase step = min(step*1.5, max_step). Else break. If none accept, decrease step *=0.5, maybe after 10 failures perturb.

But if direction has zero max (grad flat), break.

Now, Nesterov acceleration. In mirror descent, common update:
```
prev_dir = 0
for:
    grad = compute_direction(a)
    dir = beta*prev_dir + (1-beta)*grad
    prev_dir = dir
    normalize dir
```

Pseudo:
```
def smooth_mirror_descent(a0, time_budget, tau_start=1e-6, tau_end=1e-8, momentum0=0.8, step0=0.4, verbose=True):
    a = normalize(a0)
    c = conv_self(a); obj = score(a,c); best_a, best_obj
    v = zeros(n); step=step0; last_best=obj; iter=0; start=time
    while time<start+budget:
        # tau decay: tau = tau_start * (tau_end/tau_start)^(min(1, iter/max_iters)) or time-based
        # use tau_current maybe based on objective plateau.
        c = conv_self(a); obj_now=score
        if obj_now<best: update, step=min(step*1.05,max*2)
        # weights q
        gap = c.max() - np.mean(c)
        tau = max(1e-9, gap * 8.0)?? maybe schedule.
        q = softmax_from_c(c, tau)
        h = weighted_conv(q,a)
        dir = h - h.mean()
        norm = max(np.max(np.abs(dir)), eps)
        dir /= norm
        v = beta*v + (1-beta)*dir
        vnorm=norm; if small; v/=vnorm
        # line search
        accepted=False
        for alpha in backtracking from step:
             trial=apply(a,v,alpha)
             obj_trial=score(trial)
             if obj_trial < obj_now*(1-1e-12): accept; step=min(step*1.05,max); break
             else alpha*=0.5
        if not accepted:
             step*=0.7
             if too many fails: perturb/reset
        iter++
        if iter-best_iter>limit: perturb
        # periodic sym check
    return best
```

But recomputing c twice per iteration (for obj and gradient) if accept? We can use current c from previous trial. At loop start, c current. If accept, c set to c_trial. Good.

`softmax_from_c(c, tau)`:
```
L=len(c); cmax=c.max()
if tau <=0: q=zeros; q[argmax]=1; return q
x=(c-cmax)/tau
x=np.clip(x, -700, 0)
q=np.exp(x); s=q.sum()
if s==0: q=zeros; q[argmax]=1
else q/=s
return q
```

Maybe implement an alternative to LP: solve min max B y via a few projected gradient iterations. Variables y on active set. This is convex with many constraints. We can do mirror descent in y: initialize uniform. At each step, compute h = B y via matrix-vector (or FFT with q=y). Objective max_i h_i. Subgradient: select peak coordinate i*, gradient wrt y is e_{p} a_{i*?}? Actually h_i = ∑ y_p a_{i_p?}. For active coordinate i where h_i=max, gradient wrt y_p = a_{p?} (if k_p-i valid). Multiplicative y_p ∝ y_p exp(-η a_{k? - i}) for i peak. This is similar but with a and y roles swapped. We can run a few dual iterations to flatten h. Maybe cheaper and avoids LP. LP weights function:
```
def lp_dual_weights(a,K,T?):
    n=len(a)
    # Build B rows i, cols p
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for p,k in enumerate(K):
       i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
       idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1)
       rows.append(idx); cols.append(np.full(len(idx),p,dtype=int)); data.append(a[k-idx])
    rows=np.concatenate(rows)
    B=csc_matrix((data,(rows,np.concatenate(cols))), shape=(n,T))
    one_col = csr_matrix(np.full((n,1), -1.0))
    A_ub = hstack((B, one_col)).tocsr()
    A_eq = np.vstack([np.ones(T+1); A_eq[0,T]=0])
    c_obj = np.zeros(T+1); c_obj[-1]=1
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(n), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1], bounds=[(0,None)]*T+[(0,None)], method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'dual_simplex_method':1?})
    if success: y=res.x[:T]; y=np.clip(y,0,None); y/=sum
       return y
```
Need import `scipy.optimize.linprog`, `scipy.sparse`. Top-level.

    n=len(a); L=len(q); out_len=L+n-1; size=1 << (out_len-1).bit_length()

Yes adding any constant to h doesn't change after normalization: a*exp(-alpha(h+C)) / sum = a*exp(-alpha h)/sum * exp(-alpha C) / (exp(-alphaC) sum) same. So we can subtract mean(h) for numeric.
      h = h - np.mean(h)

      dir=h/maxabs
      v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*dir; maxv=np.max(abs(v)); if small; v/=maxv

      cur_obj=obj
      accepted=False
      a_trial=None; c_trial=None

          c_trial=_conv_self(trial_a)

def _lp_dual_weights(a, c, T):
    n=len(a); L=len(c)
    if T<=1: q=zeros; q[argmax]=1; return q
    T=min(T,L)
    top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -T)[-T:]
    # sort by c desc maybe for deterministic
    top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]]? This sorts only T.
    # Build B
    rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
    for p,k in enumerate(top_idx.tolist()):
       i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
       idx = np.arange(i0,i1+1)
       rows_parts.append(idx)
       cols_parts.append(np.full(idx.shape, p, dtype=np.int64))
       data_parts.append(a[k - idx])
    rows=np.concatenate(rows_parts); cols=...; data=...
    B = sp.csc_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(n,T))
    one = sp.csr_matrix(np.full((n,1), -1.0))
    A_ub = sp.hstack((B, one)).tocsr()
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,T+1)); A_eq[0,:T]=1.0
    c_obj=np.zeros(T+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    bounds=[(0.0, None)]*T; bounds.append((0.0, None))
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(n), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    if res.status==0 and res.x is not None:
       y=res.x[:T].copy(); y=np.where(y>0,y,0); s=y.sum(); if s<=0 return None; y/=s
       q=np.zeros(L); q[top_idx]=y; return q
    return None

def _lbfgs_polish(a0, budget, ref=None, taus=(1e-5,5e-6,2e-6,1e-6,5e-7), max_iter=80, verbose=True):

   if ref is None or a[ref]<=0: ref=int(np.argmax(a))
   init_a,a? We'll convert y.
   best_obj=local_score
   for tau in taus:
       if time > deadline break
       y,_ = _logratio_from_a(a, ref)
       res = minimize(_soft_logratio_objgrad, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(tau,n,ref), options={'maxiter': max_iter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxfun': 2000, 'disp': False})
       if res.success or res.x is not None:
           a_new = _a_from_logratio(res.x, ref, n)
           a_new=_normalize(a_new)
           c_new=_conv_self(a_new); obj_new=...

       # Also try res.fun? not objective original.
   return a,best_obj

def _pair_polish(a0, c0, budget, seed=0):
   a=normalize(a0); c=conv_self? assume
   obj=score; best...
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   h=_softmax_weights(...) conv; h = h - h.mean()
   donor_list = np.argpartition(np.maximum(h,0), -200)[-200:]
   recv_list = np.argpartition(h, 200)[:200]
   # add random indices to both?
   start=time; it=0
   while time<start+budget:
       # periodically recompute h every 2000 accepted? if many
       if it % 2000 ==0: recompute h
       # choose j/i
       mode = rng.random()
       if mode <0.7:
          j = donor_list[rng.integers(len(donor_list))]
          i = recv_list[rng.integers(len(recv_list))]
       else:
          i,j = rng.integers(0,n,2)
       if i==j: continue
       vi=a[i]; vj=a[j]
       if vi<1e-14 and vj<1e-14: continue
       base = min(vi,vj)
       # candidates: positive and negative fractions of base maybe with feasible limits
       fracs = [0.1,0.3,0.6,0.9]
       for f in fracs:
          # positive: move from j to i
          delta = f*base
          if delta < vj and delta > -vi:
             ct = _trial_pair_c(c,a,i,j,delta,n)
             m=ct.max()
             if 2*n*m < obj - 1e-15:
                 a[i]+=delta; a[j]-=delta; a=normalize? pair preserves sum, but floating; a/=a.sum()
                 c=ct.copy(); obj=2*n*m; if obj<best: update
                 # after accept, maybe break candidates and choose new pair? Could continue same i,j with smaller/larger? Let's break to diversify.
       # also try normalized exact? Instead of using ct from trial, if accept, recompute c fully to eliminate errors:
                 c=_conv_self(a); obj=score(c); (this may differ); if obj<best ...
       it+=1
   return best
```

```
def _trial_pair_c(c,a,i,j,delta,n):
    ct=c.copy()
    s=i; e=i+n
    if e>2*n-1: e=... but i<=n-1, e=i+n <=2n-1, okay. L=2n-1; e=min(i+n,L)
    ct[s:e] += 2.0*delta*a
    k=2*i
    if k < 2*n-1: ct[k] += delta*delta
    s=j; e=j+n; e=min(...)
    ct[s:e] -= 2.0*delta*a
    k=2*j
    if k<L: ct[k] += delta*delta
    k=i+j
    if k<L: ct[k] -= delta*delta
    return ct
```

Now, `_softmax_weights(c,tau)`: If tau is very small and cmax multiple equal, shifted 0 for all max, q uniform over them. Good.
```
def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    L=len(c); cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if L==0: return np.array([])
    if tau <= 1e-15:
       q=zeros; q[np.argmax(c)]=1; return q
    x=(c-cmax)/tau
    # To avoid all zero if x very negative, clip min -700
    x=np.where(x > -700, x, -700)
    x=np.minimum(x,0.0)
    q=np.exp(x)
    s=q.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not finite:
       q=zeros; q[np.argmax(c)]=1
    else: q/=s
    return q
```
Clipping min at -700 means far many peaks get ~1e-304, sum negligible. If all x < -700 impossible because one c=cmax gives 0.

`_weighted_conv(q,a,factor)`: q length L=2n-1. `out_len=L+n-1=3n-2`; size pow2. a_rev = a[::-1]. Convolution: `np.real(ifft(Q*R))` length out_len. The needed h length n from index n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive (n values). Check with small: n=2, a=[a0,a1], q=[q0,q1,q2]. a_rev=[a1,a0]. conv length 4. idx n-1=1 to 3: [1,2]? conv[1]=q0*a1+q1*a0? For i=0, ∑ q_k a_{k-0}: k=0 a0 + k=1 a1? Wait a_{k-i} valid 0≤k≤n-1 for i=0: k=0 a0, k=1 a1. conv with a_rev? Let's compute: conv(q,a_rev)[m] = ∑_k q_k a_rev[m-k]. For m=1: k=0 a_rev[1]=a0; k=1 a_rev[0]=a1. Good sum a0 q0 + a1 q1. But also k=2? a_rev[-1] invalid. So formula gives valid k≤m. For i=0, m=n-1=1, k=0..1, good. For i=1, m=2: k=0 a_rev[2] invalid? length 2, index 2 invalid; k=1 a_rev[1]=a0; k=2 a_rev[0]=a1 -> q1 a0 + q2 a1. Valid for i=1: k-i ∈[0,1] -> k=1 a0, k=2 a1. Good. So slice [n-1:2n-1] correct.

```
def _smooth_mirror(a0, budget, seed=0, focus_start=0.15, focus_end=0.01, momentum_start=0.85, momentum_end=0.92, alpha0=0.5, lp_interval=0, lp_T=96, max_lp_calls=0, verbose=False):
   a=_normalize(a0); n=len(a); c=_conv_self(a); obj=...; best=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
   v=np.zeros(n); alpha=alpha0; fails=0; it=0; start=time.monotonic(); rnd=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   next_print=1000
   while time.monotonic() < start+budget and obj not? :
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-start; frac=min(1, elapsed/budget)
      focus=focus_start + (focus_end-focus_start)*frac
      gap = max(c.max() - np.mean(c), 1e-12)
      tau = 1e-10 + focus*gap
      q=None
      if lp_interval>0 and it % lp_interval ==0 and it>0 and max_lp_calls>0 and time.monotonic() < start+budget-30:
          q=_lp_dual_weights(a,c,lp_T)
          # if q is None, fallback
          if q is not None: max_lp_calls-=1? Need mutable. We'll use local count.
      if q is None: q=_softmax_weights(c,tau)
      h=_weighted_conv(q,a,1.0)
      h = h - np.mean(h)
      maxh=np.max(np.abs(h)); if maxh<1e-16: break
      dir=h/maxh
      momentum=momentum_start+(momentum_end-momentum_start)*frac
      v = momentum*v + (1.0-momentum)*dir
      maxv=np.max(np.abs(v)); if maxv<1e-16: v=dir/maxh; maxv=1
      v = v/maxv
      # line search
      cur_obj=obj
      tested_alpha=alpha
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(7):
          a_trial=_normalize(a * np.exp(np.clip(-tested_alpha * v, -50, 50)))
          c_trial=_conv_self(a_trial)
          obj_trial=_score_from_c(c_trial,n)
          if obj_trial < cur_obj - 1e-14:
              a=a_trial; c=c_trial; obj=obj_trial; accepted=True; break
          tested_alpha *= 0.6
      if accepted:
          if obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
              best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=it
          alpha = min(alpha * 1.03, 3.0)
          fails=0
      else:
          alpha = max(alpha * 0.6, 0.02)
          fails += 1
      if it - last_best > 1500 or fails > 15:
          # reset to best and perturb
          a=best_a.copy()
          noise_scale = 0.05 + 0.2*frac? Maybe 0.1
          noise=rnd.standard_normal(n) * (0.05 / np.sqrt(n)?) Wait multiplicative exp noise. If noise std sigma, log changes. Use sigma = 0.1 + 0.2*(1-frac)? But too large. Let's try 0.05
          a = best_a * np.exp(rnd.standard_normal(n) * 0.05)
          a = _normalize(a)
          c=_conv_self(a); obj=score
          v*=0.2; alpha=0.5; fails=0; last_best=it
      it +=1
      if verbose and it % 2000==0: print
   return best_a,best_obj
```

Let's think of any mathematical enhancement: The LP alternating state-of-art could be implemented more directly: solve LP for g (not dual weights) using active constraints and then blend. Could yield better than subgradient. Maybe we can try a coarse version: Every certain time, solve a smaller LP for g with active peaks? For fixed f, constraints f*g ≤ M (current max). We want maximize sum g or minimize M. Instead of full, we can restrict constraints to top K active peaks (K maybe 200) and solve LP max sum g subject to f*g≤M for those constraints and g≥0. Matrix Kx n sparse with K rows from B^T (K constraints, each row length n). This is smaller (200x4096, 819k entries). Solve max sum g. This is a convex relaxation of LP (fewer constraints), but can provide a descent direction g. Then blend a ← (1-t)a + t g/t? Let's examine. If we solve `max 1^T g s.t. g≥0, (f*g)_k ≤ M for k in active set K`, the solution g has f*g low on active peaks (≤M) and sum maybe >1. Normalize to sum1 (which reduces f*g ≤M/sum? Actually if sum≥1, normalized sum1 f*g ≤ M/sum ≤ M). Then line search blend `a_new = (1-λ)a + λ g_norm`. Since f=a. We need check a_new*a_new. This is similar to Frank-Wolfe but g optimized over linear constraints. Could be effective. We can solve this LP with K active constraints; variables n. K=200, constraints 200, dense-ish. HiGHS should be fast. It may push mass to regions where f coefficients low, creating a different shape. Blending may reduce peaks.

The state-of-art used all constraints, but active top K likely binding. This is different from dual weights; it optimizes g, not weights. Could outperform subgradient. Let's design this as an "alternating LP direction" step.

Given a=f normalized sum1, current M=max(f*f). Select K active peaks (top K c). Solve:
max ∑ g_i
s.t. g>=0
∑_{i} f_{k-i} g_i ≤ M for k in K
(no sum constraint). Since g can scale unbounded? Constraints with nonnegative f, if there exists g with zero constraint? If sum objective, constraints bound it. M current positive. f has positive entries? If some f zeros maybe. Feasible g=f (sum1) because constraints f*f≤M. LP finite. We can solve with linprog: minimize -sum g, A_ub = B_active (K x n) with b=M, bounds g≥0. Matrix B_active rows for selected k: row values f[k-i] for valid i (else 0). This is a row-sparse matrix. For K=200, entries ~K*n/2? constraints each length up to n, total ~800k. Easy. No need t variable.
Then normalize g to sum1. Use as direction: `a_new = (1-λ)a + λ g_norm` (and nonnegative). Line search λ∈[0,1] that reduces objective. The g_norm may be wild but convex combo with a.

This is a natural descent direction from linearized constraints and previous SOTA. Let's incorporate! It might be more powerful than mirror.

LP for g details:
```
def _solve_g_lp(a, K, M, n):
    # build B sparse K x n
    rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(K):
       i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
       idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1)
       rows_parts.append(np.full(len(idx), r, dtype=int)); cols_parts.append(idx); data_parts.append(a[k-idx])
    B=sp.csc_matrix((data,(rows,cols)),shape=(K,n))
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=np.full(K, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
    if success: g=res.x; g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s
```

Pseudo code complete:

```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
import scipy.sparse as sp

def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s

def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    L = 2*n - 1
    size = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:L]
    # clean tiny imaginary? already real
    return c

def _score_from_c(c, n):
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0: mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)

def _score(a):
    n=len(a); c=_conv_self(a); return _score_from_c(c,n)

def _softmax_weights(c, tau): ...

def _weighted_conv(q, a, factor=1.0): ...

def _apply_mirror(a, direction, alpha):
    a_trial = a * np.exp(np.clip(-alpha * direction, -50.0, 50.0))
    return _normalize(a_trial)

def _smooth_mirror(...): ...
```
In `_smooth_mirror`, need access to `_lp_dual_weights`. But if `_lp_dual_weights` uses linprog, maybe not imported if error. We can wrap.

Let's implement `_lp_dual_weights`. If `linprog` raises, return None.
```
def _lp_dual_weights(a,c,T):
    try:
        n=a.size; L=c.size
        if T<=1:
            q=np.zeros(L); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return q
        T = int(min(T,L))
        if T <=0: return None
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -T)[-T:]
        order = np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]
        top_idx = top_idx[order]
        rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
        for p,k in enumerate(top_idx.tolist()):
            i0 = k - (n - 1)
            if i0 < 0: i0 = 0
            i1 = k
            if i1 > n - 1: i1 = n - 1
            if i1 < i0: continue
            idx = np.arange(i0, i1+1, dtype=np.int64)
            rows_parts.append(idx)
            cols_parts.append(np.full(idx.shape, p, dtype=np.int64))
            data_parts.append(a[k - idx])
        if not rows_parts: return None
        rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
        cols = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
        data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
        if rows.size == 0: return None
        B = sp.csc_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(n, T))
        one = sp.csr_matrix(np.full((n,1), -1.0))
        A_ub = sp.hstack((B, one)).tocsr()
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, T+1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :T] = 1.0
        c_obj = np.zeros(T+1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (T+1)
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
        if res is None or res.status != 0 or res.x is None: return None
        y = np.asarray(res.x[:T], dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.where(y > 0.0, y, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
        q=np.zeros(L); q[top_idx]=y/s; return q
    except Exception:
        return None
```
Note: In LP, B row i col p = a[k-i]. We used `idx` from i0 to i1, i=k-d with d=k-i ∈ [0,n-1]. `a[k-idx]`: if idx i0..i1, k-i ranges. For i0=max(0,k-n+1), k-i ≤ n-1; for i1=min(n-1,k), k-i≥0. Good.

```
def _solve_g_lp(a, top_idx, M):
    try:
       n=a.size; K=len(top_idx)
       if K==0: return None
       rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
       for r,k in enumerate(top_idx.tolist()):
           i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
           idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1,dtype=int)
           rows_parts.append(np.full(idx.shape,r,dtype=int)); cols_parts.append(idx); data_parts.append(a[k-idx])
       B=sp.csc_matrix((data,(rows,cols)),shape=(K,n))
       c_obj=-np.ones(n)
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=np.full(K, M), bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       if success: c_obj?; g=res.x; g=np.clip(g,0,None); s=sum; if s>0: return g/s
    except: return None

def _g_lp_optimize(a0, budget, K=160, max_calls=5, verbose=False):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; c=_conv_self(a); obj=_score_from_c(c,n); best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    start=time.monotonic(); call=0
    while call < max_calls and time.monotonic() < start+budget:
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0: break
        g = _solve_g_lp(a, top_idx, M*1.000001)
        if g is not None:
            best_trial_obj=obj; best_trial_a=None; best_trial_c=None; best_lam=0.0
            # lambdas order maybe lower first? We evaluate all
            for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0):
                t_np = (1.0-lam)*a + lam*g
                t_np = _normalize(t_np)
                ct = _conv_self(t_np)
                ot = _score_from_c(ct,n)
                if ot < best_trial_obj - 1e-14:
                    best_trial_obj=ot; best_trial_a=t_np; best_trial_c=ct; best_lam=lam
            if best_trial_a is not None:
                a=best_trial_a; c=best_trial_c; obj=best_trial_obj
                if obj < best_obj:
                    best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy()
                    if verbose: print(...)
        call +=1
    return best_a,best_obj
```

Now, `_lbfgs_polish` code:
```
def _a_from_logratio(y, ref, orig, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    m = max(0.0, float(np.max(y))) if y.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(y - m)
    denom = 1.0 + float(np.sum(e))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a.fill(np.exp(-m) / denom)
    # Actually set all to ref value, then override orig
    a[orig] = e / denom
    a[ref] = np.exp(-m) / denom
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return _normalize(np.ones(n))
    return a/s

def _logratio_from_a(a, ref=None):
    n=a.size
    if ref is None or ref<0 or ref>=n or a[ref] <= 1e-300:
        ref=int(np.argmax(a))
    ref_val=float(a[ref])
    if ref_val <= 1e-300:
        return None, None
    orig=np.arange(n); orig=orig[orig != ref]
    vals = a[orig]
    y = np.empty_like(vals)
    mask = vals > 1e-300
    y[~mask] = -50.0
    y[mask] = np.log(vals[mask] / ref_val)
    # clip for numerical
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 80.0)
    return y, ref, orig

def _soft_logratio_objgrad(z, tau, n, ref, orig):
    # stable a
    if z.size != n-1: return (1e100, np.zeros(z.size))
    m = max(0.0, float(np.max(z)))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    denom = 1.0 + float(np.sum(e))
    a = np.empty(n); a.fill(np.exp(-m)/denom); a[orig]=e/denom; a[ref]=np.exp(-m)/denom
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0: ...
    a = a/s
    c = _conv_self(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 0: tau=1e-9
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    shifted = np.where(shifted > -700.0, shifted, -700.0)
    shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
    q = np.exp(shifted)
    qs = float(np.sum(q))
    if qs <= 1e-300:
       q = np.zeros(c.size); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; f=0.0
    else:
       q = q/qs; f = float(np.log(qs))
    h = _weighted_conv(q, a, 2.0)
    E = h / tau
    mu = float(np.dot(a, E))
    g = a * (E - mu)
    grad = g[orig]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return f, grad
```
Gradient scale: E=h/tau, h includes factor 2. Good.
If tau extremely small, E huge; grad may overflow. Clip grad:
```
maxg=np.max(np.abs(grad)); if maxg>1e6: grad*=1e6/maxg
```
Objective f maybe finite.

```
def _lbfgs_polish(a0, budget, ref=None, taus=(2e-5,1e-5,5e-6,2e-6,1e-6,5e-7), max_iter=60, verbose=True):
   a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; c=conv; obj=score; best...
   y, ref, orig = ...
   start=time.monotonic()
   for tau in taus:
       if time.monotonic() >= start+budget: break
       # update y from a
       y, ref, orig = _logratio_from_a(a, ref)
       if y is None: break
       try:
           res = minimize(_soft_logratio_objgrad, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(tau,n,ref,orig), options={...})
           if res.x is not None:
              a_new = _a_from_logratio(res.x, ref, orig, n)
              a_new = _normalize(a_new)
              c_new = _conv_self(a_new); obj_new=score
              if obj_new < best_obj:
                  a=a_new; c=c_new; obj=obj_new; best_obj=obj_new; best_a=a_new.copy(); print
       except Exception as e: print
   return best_a,best_obj

def _pair_polish(a0, budget, seed=1, verbose=False):

       improved_pair=False
       for f in fracs:
           for sign in (1, -1):
               delta = sign * f * base
               if delta > -vi + 1e-15 and delta < vj - 1e-15:
                   ct = _trial_pair_c(c,a,i,j,delta,n)
                   ot = _score_from_c(ct,n)
                   if ot < obj - 1e-14:
                       # accept immediately, apply to a/c
                       a[i] += delta; a[j] -= delta
                       # renormalize? pair preserves sum but due floating:
                       a_sum = a.sum(); if a_sum>0: a = a/a_sum
                       c = _conv_self(a)
                       obj = _score_from_c(c,n)
                       if obj < best_obj:
                           best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); accept_count+=1
                           if verbose and accept_count%100==0: print
                       improved_pair=True
                       break
           if improved_pair: break
       it += 1
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
import scipy.sparse as sp

def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out=np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        return out/out.sum()
    arr=arr/s
    if floor > 0.0:
        arr=np.maximum(arr, floor)
        s2=arr.sum()
        if s2 > 0: arr=arr/s2
    return arr
```
If input empty? arr.size=0, s=0 -> fallback 256. But if given empty invalid. Good.

`_conv_self`: for n maybe 1, L=1, size = 1 << (0).bit_length()? `(1-1).bit_length()` = 0.bit_length() =0, 1<<0=1. fft length1 okay. Good.

`_score_from_c`: if n=0? no.

`_softmax_weights`: Need if L maybe 0; return empty.
```
def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.zeros(0)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        q=np.zeros(L); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return q
    x=(c-cmax)/tau
    x=np.where(x > -700.0, x, -700.0)
    x=np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    q=np.exp(x)
    s=float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       q=np.zeros(L); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else: q/=s
    return q
```
If c contains NaN? from conv no. But if, cmax nan. Use `if not np.isfinite(cmax):` return argmax after nan_to_num? We can sanitize c inside score? Maybe not. Use `c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)` in `_conv_self`? We can after c[:L], set c[~finite]=0. Add:
```
c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
```
Good.

`_weighted_conv`:
```
def _weighted_conv(q, a, factor=1.0):
    n=a.size; L=q.size
    out_len = L + n - 1
    size = 1 << (out_len - 1).bit_length()
    Q=fft(q, size)
    R=fft(a[::-1], size)
    conv=np.real(ifft(Q*R))
    conv=np.where(np.isfinite(conv), conv, 0.0)
    return factor * conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If n=1, L=1, out_len=1, slice [0:1] good.

`_apply_mirror`:
```
def _apply_mirror(a, direction, alpha):
    w = -alpha * direction
    w = np.clip(w, -50.0, 50.0)
    trial = a * np.exp(w)
    return _normalize(trial, 1e-15)
```

`_smooth_mirror` code:
Need args. We'll implement with local variables.
```
def _smooth_mirror(a0, budget, seed=123, focus_start=0.15, focus_end=0.01, momentum_start=0.85, momentum_end=0.92, alpha0=0.5, lp_interval=0, lp_T=96, max_lp_calls=0, verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a0, 1e-15)
    n=a.size
    c = _conv_self(a)
    obj = _score_from_c(c,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj; last_best=0
    v=np.zeros(n); alpha=alpha0; fails=0; it=0; lp_used=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
       while time.monotonic() < start + budget:
          elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
          frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(1e-9,budget))
          focus = focus_start + (focus_end-focus_start)*frac
          cmax=float(np.max(c)); mean=float(np.mean(c))
          gap = cmax - mean
          if gap <= 1e-15: gap = max(cmax,1e-15)
          tau = 1e-10 + focus*gap
          q=None
          if lp_interval > 0 and it % lp_interval == 0 and lp_used < max_lp_calls and time.monotonic() < start + budget - 30:
              q = _lp_dual_weights(a,c,lp_T)
              lp_used += 1
          if q is None:
              q = _softmax_weights(c,tau)
          h = _weighted_conv(q,a,1.0)
          h = h - np.mean(h)
          maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
          if maxh < 1e-16:
             break
          direction = h / maxh
          momentum = momentum_start + (momentum_end - momentum_start)*frac
          v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * direction
          maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
          if maxv < 1e-16:
              v = direction
              maxv = 1.0
          else:
              v = v / maxv
          cur_obj = obj
          accepted=False
          try_alpha = alpha
          for _ in range(7):
              trial = _apply_mirror(a, v, try_alpha)
              c_trial = _conv_self(trial)
              obj_trial = _score_from_c(c_trial,n)
              if obj_trial < cur_obj - 1e-12:
                  a=trial; c=c_trial; obj=obj_trial; accepted=True; break
              try_alpha *= 0.6
          if accepted:
              if obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
                  best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=it
              alpha = min(alpha*1.04, 3.0)
              fails = 0
          else:
              alpha = max(alpha*0.6, 0.02)
              fails += 1
          if it - last_best > 1500 or fails > 15:
              a = best_a.copy()
              # random multiplicative perturbation
              noise = np.random.default_rng(seed + it).standard_normal(n) * 0.05
              a = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -3.0, 3.0))
              a = _normalize(a, 1e-15)
              c = _conv_self(a)
              obj = _score_from_c(c,n)
              v = v * 0.2
              alpha = 0.4
              fails = 0
              last_best = it
          it += 1
          if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
              print(f"    smooth_mirror: it={it}, obj={obj:.8f}, best={best_obj:.8f}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s")
    except Exception as e:
       if verbose: print("smooth_mirror error:", e)
    return best_a, best_obj

def _g_lp_optimize(a0, budget, K=160, max_calls=5, verbose=False):
    a=_normalize(a0,1e-15); n=a.size; c=_conv_self(a); obj=...
    best...
    start=time.monotonic(); call=0
    while call < max_calls and time.monotonic() < start+budget:
        Kcur = min(K, c.size)
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -Kcur)[-Kcur:]
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0: break
        g = _solve_g_lp(a, top_idx, M * (1.0 + 1e-9))
        if g is not None:
            best_trial_obj = obj; best_trial_a=None; best_trial_c=None
            for lam in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
                trial = (1.0-lam)*a + lam*g
                trial = _normalize(trial, 1e-15)
                ct = _conv_self(trial)
                ot = _score_from_c(ct,n)
                if ot < best_trial_obj - 1e-14:
                    best_trial_obj=ot; best_trial_a=trial; best_trial_c=ct
            if best_trial_a is not None:
                a=best_trial_a; c=best_trial_c; obj=best_trial_obj
                if obj < best_obj:
                    best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy()
                    if verbose: print(...)
        call += 1
    return best_a,best_obj

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    print timings

          g = np.asarray(given_arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

             given_a = g / g.sum()

               ev = ov

           ev = ov

       else: best_final=np.ones(4096)
    # sanitize
    final = np.asarray(best_final,float).reshape(-1)
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.clip(final,0,1000)
    s=final.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
       final=np.ones(4096)
    else:
       final=final/s
    # optional remove tiny negatives already. Do not floor.
    # final exact verify and if fallback
    try:
       ev=evaluate_sequence(final.tolist())
    except Exception:
       ev=global_best
    if not np.isfinite(ev):
       # fallback to given or ones
       if given_a is not None:
           final=_normalize(given_a,0.0)
       else:
           final=np.ones(4096)
    print final
    return final.tolist()
```

```
except: total_budget=2400
safety = min(60.0, max(1.0, total_budget*0.02))
deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - safety)
```

```python
"""
Optimize a non-negative step function to minimize

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The search combines:
  - smoothed mirror descent with full softmax peak weights,
  - small active-set LP duals for gradient weights,
  - Frank-Wolfe-style LP directions minimizing max(f*g) on active peaks,
  - L-BFGS-B polishing in a log-ratio parameterization of the simplex,
  - pair-mass incremental convolution polishing.

The returned list is chosen by exact evaluation among the best local candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
import scipy.sparse as sp


def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    """Normalize a to sum 1 and enforce non-negative finite values."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        return out / out.sum()
    arr = arr / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        arr = np.maximum(arr, float(floor))
        s2 = float(arr.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
            arr = arr / s2
    return arr


def _conv_self(a):
    """Full self-convolution via FFT."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    length = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << (length - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(arr, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:length]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score_from_c(c, n):
    """Score from convolution coefficients c and sequence length n."""
    if c.size == 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    """Stable softmax probabilities over convolution peaks."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        q = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    x = (c - cmax) / tau
    x = np.where(x > -700.0, x, -700.0)
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    q = np.exp(x)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    q = q / s
    return q


def _weighted_conv(q, a, factor=1.0):
    """
    Compute grad_i = factor * sum_k q_k * a_{k-i} for i=0..n-1.
    q has length 2n-1.
    """
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = arr.size
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    length_q = q.size
    out_len = length_q + n - 1
    size = 1 << (out_len - 1).bit_length()
    Q = fft(q, size)
    R = fft(arr[::-1], size)
    conv = np.real(ifft(Q * R))
    conv = np.where(np.isfinite(conv), conv, 0.0)
    return factor * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _apply_mirror(a, direction, alpha):
    """Multiplicative step on the simplex."""
    w = -float(alpha) * direction
    w = np.clip(w, -50.0, 50.0)
    trial = a * np.exp(w)
    return _normalize(trial, 1e-15)


def _lp_dual_weights(a, c, T):
    """
    Solve a small LP over active peaks:

        min t
        s.t. B y <= t 1,  sum(y) = 1, y >= 0

    where B_{i,k} = a_{k-i}.  Returns weights q over the selected peaks.
    """
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        n = arr.size
        L = int(np.asarray(c).size)
        if n > 8192:
            return None
        T = int(min(T, L))
        if T <= 0:
            return None
        if T == 1:
            q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            return q

        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -T)[-T:]
        order = np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]
        top_idx = top_idx[order]

        rows_parts = []
        cols_parts = []
        data_parts = []

        for p, k in enumerate(top_idx.tolist()):
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i1 < i0:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            rows_parts.append(idx)
            cols_parts.append(np.full(idx.shape, p, dtype=np.int64))
            data_parts.append(arr[k - idx])

        if not rows_parts:
            return None

        rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
        cols = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
        data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
        if rows.size == 0:
            return None

        B = sp.csc_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(n, T))
        one_col = sp.csr_matrix(np.full((n, 1), -1.0, dtype=np.float64))
        A_ub = sp.hstack((B, one_col)).tocsr()

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, T + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :T] = 1.0

        c_obj = np.zeros(T + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (T + 1)

        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )

        if res is None or res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
            return None

        y = np.asarray(res.x[:T], dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.where(y > 0.0, y, 0.0)
        s = float(y.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None

        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[top_idx] = y / s
        return q
    except Exception:
        return None


def _smooth_mirror(
    a0,
    budget,
    seed=123,
    focus_start=0.15,
    focus_end=0.01,
    momentum_start=0.85,
    momentum_end=0.92,
    alpha0=0.5,
    lp_interval=0,
    lp_T=96,
    max_lp_calls=0,
    verbose=False
):
    """
    Smoothed mirror descent on the simplex.

    The subgradient is a softmax mixture of active convolution peaks.  LP dual
    weights are used occasionally when lp_interval > 0.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0, 1e-15)
    n = a.size
    c = _conv_self(a)
    obj = _score_from_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj
    last_best = 0

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha = float(alpha0)
    fails = 0
    it = 0
    lp_used = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget)

    try:
        while time.monotonic() < start + budget:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
            frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, budget))
            focus = focus_start + (focus_end - focus_start) * frac

            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            cmean = float(np.mean(c))
            gap = cmax - cmean
            if gap <= 1e-15:
                gap = max(cmax, 1e-15)
            tau = 1e-10 + focus * gap

            q = None
            if (lp_interval > 0 and it % lp_interval == 0 and
                    lp_used < max_lp_calls and
                    time.monotonic() < start + budget - 30.0):
                q = _lp_dual_weights(a, c, lp_T)
                lp_used += 1

            if q is None:
                q = _softmax_weights(c, tau)

            h = _weighted_conv(q, a, 1.0)
            h = h - float(np.mean(h))
            maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
            if maxh < 1e-16:
                break

            direction = h / maxh
            momentum = momentum_start + (momentum_end - momentum_start) * frac
            v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * direction
            maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
            if maxv < 1e-16:
                v = direction.copy()
                maxv = 1.0
            else:
                v = v / maxv

            cur_obj = obj
            accepted = False
            try_alpha = alpha
            for _ in range(7):
                trial = _apply_mirror(a, v, try_alpha)
                c_trial = _conv_self(trial)
                obj_trial = _score_from_c(c_trial, n)
                if obj_trial < cur_obj - 1e-13:
                    a = trial
                    c = c_trial
                    obj = obj_trial
                    accepted = True
                    break
                try_alpha *= 0.6

            if accepted:
                if obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
                    best_obj = obj
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    last_best = it
                alpha = min(alpha * 1.04, 3.0)
                fails = 0
            else:
                alpha = max(alpha * 0.60, 0.02)
                fails += 1

            if it - last_best > 1500 or fails > 15:
                a = best_a.copy()
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.05
                a = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -3.0, 3.0))
                a = _normalize(a, 1e-15)
                c = _conv_self(a)
                obj = _score_from_c(c, n)
                v = v * 0.2
                alpha = 0.4
                fails = 0
                last_best = it

            it += 1
            if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
                print(
                    f"    smooth_mirror: it={it}, obj={obj:.8f}, "
                    f"best={best_obj:.8f}, t={elapsed:.1f}s"
                )
    except Exception as e:
        if verbose:
            print("    smooth_mirror error:", e)

    return best_a, best_obj


def _solve_g_lp(a, top_idx, M):
    """
    Given fixed a=f, solve:

        max sum g
        s.t. g >= 0,
             (f*g)_k <= M for k in active peaks.

    Returns g normalized to sum 1, or None.
    """
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        n = arr.size
        K = len(top_idx)
        if n > 8192 or K == 0:
            return None

        rows_parts = []
        cols_parts = []
        data_parts = []

        for r, k in enumerate(top_idx.tolist()):
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i1 < i0:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            rows_parts.append(np.full(idx.shape, r, dtype=np.int64))
            cols_parts.append(idx)
            data_parts.append(arr[k - idx])

        if not rows_parts:
            return None

        rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
        cols = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
        data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
        if rows.size == 0:
            return None

        B = sp.csc_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(K, n))
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(K, float(M) * 1.000000001, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=B,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )

        if res is None or res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
            return None

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None
        return g / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _g_lp_optimize(a0, budget, K=160, max_calls=5, verbose=False):
    """
    Frank-Wolfe-style alternating LP step:
    solve max sum g subject to active f*g <= M, then line-search blends.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0, 1e-15)
    n = a.size
    c = _conv_self(a)
    obj = _score_from_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget)
    call = 0

    while call < max_calls and time.monotonic() < start + budget:
        Kcur = int(min(K, c.size))
        if Kcur <= 0 or n > 8192:
            break
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -Kcur)[-Kcur:]
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        g = _solve_g_lp(a, top_idx, M)
        if g is not None:
            best_trial_obj = obj
            best_trial_a = None
            best_trial_c = None

            for lam in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00):
                trial = (1.0 - lam) * a + lam * g
                trial = _normalize(trial, 1e-15)
                ct = _conv_self(trial)
                ot = _score_from_c(ct, n)
                if ot < best_trial_obj - 1e-14:
                    best_trial_obj = ot
                    best_trial_a = trial
                    best_trial_c = ct

            if best_trial_a is not None:
                a = best_trial_a
                c = best_trial_c
                obj = best_trial_obj
                if obj < best_obj:
                    best_obj = obj
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print(
                            f"    g_lp_optimize: call={call}, obj={obj:.8f}, "
                            f"best={best_obj:.8f}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s"
                        )
        call += 1

    return best_a, best_obj


def _a_from_logratio(y, ref, orig, n):
    """Reconstruct simplex vector from logits relative to fixed reference."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    if y.size:
        m = max(0.0, float(np.max(y)))
    else:
        m = 0.0
    e = np.exp(y - m)
    denom = 1.0 + float(np.sum(e))
    if denom <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(denom):
        return _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a.fill(np.exp(-m) / denom)
    if orig.size:
        a[orig] = e / denom
    a[ref] = np.exp(-m) / denom
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    return a / s


def _logratio_from_a(a, ref=None):
    """Convert simplex vector to log-ratio variables with one fixed reference."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if ref is None or ref < 0 or ref >= n or a[ref] <= 1e-300:
        ref = int(np.argmax(a))
    ref_val = float(a[ref])
    if ref_val <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ref_val):
        return None, None, None

    orig = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    orig = orig[orig != ref]
    vals = a[orig]
    y = np.empty(vals.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = vals > 1e-300
    y[~mask] = -50.0
    if np.any(mask):
        y[mask] = np.log(vals[mask] / ref_val)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 80.0)
    return y, ref, orig


def _soft_logratio_objgrad(z, tau, n, ref, orig):
    """
    Objective and gradient for L-BFGS-B:

        f(a) = logsumexp(conv(a,a) / tau)

    parameterized by log-ratio variables with one logit fixed.  This is
    consistent with gradient E = 2 * conv(q, a) / tau, where q is the softmax
    peak distribution.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if z.size != n - 1:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)

    if z.size:
        m = max(0.0, float(np.max(z)))
    else:
        m = 0.0
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    denom = 1.0 + float(np.sum(e))
    if denom <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(denom):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a.fill(np.exp(-m) / denom)
    if orig.size:
        a[orig] = e / denom
    a[ref] = np.exp(-m) / denom
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s

    c = _conv_self(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = float(tau)
    if tau <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    x = (c - cmax) / tau
    x = np.where(x > -700.0, x, -700.0)
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)

    q = np.exp(x)
    qs = float(q.sum())
    if qs <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(qs):
        q = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        f = cmax / tau
    else:
        q = q / qs
        f = cmax / tau + float(np.log(qs))

    h = _weighted_conv(q, a, 2.0)
    E = h / tau
    mu = float(np.dot(a, E))
    g_full = a * (E - mu)
    grad = g_full[orig]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
    if maxg > 1e6:
        grad = grad * (1e6 / maxg)

    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = np.inf
    return float(f), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _lbfgs_polish(a0, budget, ref=None, taus=(2e-5, 1e-5, 5e-6, 2e-6, 1e-6),
                  max_iter=60, verbose=False):
    """
    Polish on the simplex using L-BFGS-B and a stabilized log-sum-exp proxy
    for the maximum convolution coefficient.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0, 1e-15)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, _score_from_c(_conv_self(a), n)

    c = _conv_self(a)
    obj = _score_from_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget)

    for tau in taus:
        if time.monotonic() >= start + budget:
            break

        y, ref, orig = _logratio_from_a(a, ref)
        if y is None:
            break

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _soft_logratio_objgrad,
                y,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                args=(float(tau), n, int(ref), orig),
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(max_iter),
                    "ftol": 1e-10,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxfun": 1200,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": False
                }
            )

            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                a_new = _a_from_logratio(res.x, ref, orig, n)
                a_new = _normalize(a_new, 1e-15)
                c_new = _conv_self(a_new)
                obj_new = _score_from_c(c_new, n)
                if obj_new < best_obj - 1e-14:
                    a = a_new
                    c = c_new
                    obj = obj_new
                    best_obj = obj_new
                    best_a = a_new.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print(
                            f"    lbfgs_tau={tau:.2e}: obj={obj:.8f}, "
                            f"best={best_obj:.8f}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s"
                        )
        except Exception as e:
            if verbose:
                print("    lbfgs error:", e)

    return best_a, best_obj


def _trial_pair_c(c, a, i, j, delta, n):
    """Exact O(n) update of convolution after moving mass from j to i."""
    ct = c.copy()
    L = 2 * n - 1

    s = i
    e = min(i + n, L)
    ct[s:e] += 2.0 * delta * a

    k = 2 * i
    if 0 <= k < L:
        ct[k] += delta * delta

    s = j
    e = min(j + n, L)
    ct[s:e] -= 2.0 * delta * a

    k = 2 * j
    if 0 <= k < L:
        ct[k] += delta * delta

    k = i + j
    if 0 <= k < L:
        ct[k] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

    return ct


def _pair_polish(a0, budget, seed=1, verbose=False):
    """
    Very local polish: move a small mass between two coordinates and accept
    if the maximum convolution coefficient decreases.  Convolution updates
    are done incrementally for the trial.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0, 1e-15)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, _score_from_c(_conv_self(a), n)

    c = _conv_self(a)
    obj = _score_from_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    def _recompute_h():
        pass

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget)
    it = 0
    accept_count = 0
    listsize = min(200, n)

    gap = float(max(0.0, np.max(c) - np.mean(c)))
    tau = 1e-8 + 0.05 * max(gap, 1e-15)
    q = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
    h = _weighted_conv(q, a, 1.0)
    h = h - float(np.mean(h))

    if listsize == n:
        donors = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        receivers = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        donors = np.argpartition(-h, listsize)[:listsize]
        receivers = np.argpartition(h, listsize)[:listsize]

    fracs = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.65, 0.90)

    while time.monotonic() < start + budget:
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            gap = float(max(0.0, np.max(c) - np.mean(c)))
            tau = 1e-8 + 0.05 * max(gap, 1e-15)
            q = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
            h = _weighted_conv(q, a, 1.0)
            h = h - float(np.mean(h))
            if listsize == n:
                donors = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
                receivers = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
            else:
                donors = np.argpartition(-h, listsize)[:listsize]
                receivers = np.argpartition(h, listsize)[:listsize]

        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            j = int(donors[rng.integers(donors.size)])
            i = int(receivers[rng.integers(receivers.size)])
        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))

        if i == j:
            it += 1
            continue

        vi = float(a[i])
        vj = float(a[j])
        base = min(vi, vj)
        if base <= 1e-14:
            it += 1
            continue

        improved_pair = False
        for f in fracs:
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                delta = sign * f * base
                if delta <= -vi + 1e-14 or delta >= vj - 1e-14:
                    continue

                ct = _trial_pair_c(c, a, i, j, delta, n)
                ot = _score_from_c(ct, n)

                if ot < obj - 1e-14:
                    a[i] += delta
                    a[j] -= delta
                    a = _normalize(a, 0.0)
                    c = _conv_self(a)
                    obj = _score_from_c(c, n)

                    if obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
                        best_obj = obj
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        accept_count += 1
                        if verbose and accept_count % 200 == 0:
                            print(
                                f"    pair_polish: accepts={accept_count}, "
                                f"obj={obj:.8f}, best={best_obj:.8f}, "
                                f"t={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s"
                            )
                    improved_pair = True
                    break
            if improved_pair:
                break

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing the provided
    evaluation function.  Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    safety = min(60.0, max(1.0, total_budget * 0.02))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - safety)

    global_a = None
    global_best = np.inf
    given_a = None

    print(f"propose_candidate: budget={total_budget:.1f}s, safety={safety:.1f}s")

    raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if raw is not None:
        try:
            g = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if g.size > 0 and g.sum() > 0.01:
                given_a = g / g.sum()
                c = _conv_self(given_a)
                obj = _score_from_c(c, given_a.size)
                if np.isfinite(obj):
                    global_a = given_a.copy()
                    global_best = obj
                    print(f"initial given: n={given_a.size}, local_obj={obj:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("could not load given sequence:", e)

    if global_a is None:
        n = 4096
        i = np.arange(n)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        cands = []

        cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

        ax = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 7.0 * i / float(n))
        cands.append(np.maximum(ax, 0.01))

        ax = 1.0 + 0.4 * np.cos(np.pi * i / float(max(1, n - 1)))
        cands.append(np.maximum(ax, 0.01))

        cands.append(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)))
        cands.append(rng.random(n) + 0.05)

        best_obj = np.inf
        best_a = None
        for cand in cands:
            arr = _normalize(cand, 1e-15)
            c = _conv_self(arr)
            obj = _score_from_c(c, arr.size)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = arr.copy()

        if best_a is not None:
            global_a = best_a
            global_best = best_obj
            given_a = best_a.copy()
            print(f"fallback initial: n={global_a.size}, local_obj={global_best:.8f}")

    if global_a is None:
        global_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        global_a = global_a / global_a.sum()
        global_best = _score_from_c(_conv_self(global_a), global_a.size)

    global_a = _normalize(global_a, 1e-15)

    # Cheap symmetrization check.
    sym = 0.5 * (global_a + global_a[::-1])
    sym = _normalize(sym, 1e-15)
    sym_obj = _score_from_c(_conv_self(sym), sym.size)
    if np.isfinite(sym_obj) and sym_obj < global_best:
        global_a = sym.copy()
        global_best = sym_obj
        print(f"symmetrized initial improves: {global_best:.8f}")
    else:
        global_a = _normalize(global_a, 1e-15)

    # Phase 1: broad smoothed mirror descent, occasional dual-weight LP.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t1 = min(0.40 * time_left, 1200.0)
    if t1 > 30.0 and global_a.size <= 16384:
        print(f"phase 1: smooth mirror for {t1:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _smooth_mirror(
            global_a, t1,
            seed=int(seed) + 1,
            focus_start=0.15,
            focus_end=0.01,
            momentum_start=0.85,
            momentum_end=0.92,
            alpha0=0.5,
            lp_interval=700,
            lp_T=96,
            max_lp_calls=4,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 1 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")
    else:
        a2, o2 = global_a, global_best

    # Phase 2: active-set LP direction in g-space.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t2 = min(0.15 * time_left, 300.0)
    if t2 > 30.0 and global_a.size <= 8192:
        print(f"phase 2: g-LP for {t2:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _g_lp_optimize(
            global_a, t2,
            K=200,
            max_calls=4,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 2 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")

    # Phase 3: L-BFGS polish.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t3 = min(0.20 * time_left, 500.0)
    if t3 > 30.0 and global_a.size <= 16384:
        print(f"phase 3: lbfgs polish for {t3:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _lbfgs_polish(
            global_a, t3,
            taus=(2e-5, 1e-5, 5e-6, 2e-6, 1e-6),
            max_iter=60,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 3 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")

    # Phase 4: sharper mirror descent.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t4 = min(0.20 * time_left, 500.0)
    if t4 > 30.0 and global_a.size <= 16384:
        print(f"phase 4: sharp smooth mirror for {t4:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _smooth_mirror(
            global_a, t4,
            seed=int(seed) + 11,
            focus_start=0.05,
            focus_end=0.005,
            momentum_start=0.90,
            momentum_end=0.95,
            alpha0=0.35,
            lp_interval=0,
            lp_T=0,
            max_lp_calls=0,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 4 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")

    # Phase 5: final short L-BFGS.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t5 = min(0.08 * time_left, 150.0)
    if t5 > 30.0 and global_a.size <= 16384:
        print(f"phase 5: final lbfgs for {t5:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _lbfgs_polish(
            global_a, t5,
            taus=(5e-6, 2e-6, 1e-6, 5e-7),
            max_iter=40,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 5 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")

    # Phase 6: local pair polish.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t6 = max(0.0, time_left - 80.0)
    if t6 > 20.0 and global_a.size <= 16384:
        print(f"phase 6: pair polish for {t6:.0f}s")
        a2, o2 = _pair_polish(global_a, t6, seed=int(seed) + 21, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < global_best:
            global_a = a2
            global_best = o2
        print(f"phase 6 done: local_best={global_best:.8f}")

    # Symmetrize final candidate if it helps.
    sym = 0.5 * (global_a + global_a[::-1])
    sym = _normalize(sym, 0.0)
    sym_obj = _score_from_c(_conv_self(sym), sym.size)
    if np.isfinite(sym_obj) and sym_obj < global_best:
        global_a = sym.copy()
        global_best = sym_obj

    # Collect candidates and choose by exact evaluator when practical.
    candidates = []
    if global_a is not None:
        candidates.append(global_a)
    if given_a is not None:
        candidates.append(given_a)

    scored_candidates = []
    for cand in candidates:
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 0.01:
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        scored_candidates.append((arr, _score_from_c(_conv_self(arr), arr.size)))

        symc = 0.5 * (arr + arr[::-1])
        symc = _normalize(symc, 0.0)
        scored_candidates.append((symc, _score_from_c(_conv_self(symc), symc.size)))

    # Remove very obvious duplicates by local score only if very few remain.
    if len(scored_candidates) > 1:
        seen = set()
        uniq = []
        for arr, val in scored_candidates:
            try:
                key = (arr.size, tuple(np.round(arr, 12).tolist()))
            except Exception:
                key = None
            if key is not None and key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            uniq.append((arr, val))
        scored_candidates = uniq

    final_exact_best = np.inf
    final_arr = None

    # Evaluate exact top candidates.
    to_evaluate = min(8, len(scored_candidates)) if scored_candidates else 0
    remaining = scored_candidates[:]
    for _ in range(to_evaluate):
        best_idx = 0
        best_val = remaining[0][1]
        for idx in range(1, len(remaining)):
            if remaining[idx][1] < best_val:
                best_val = remaining[idx][1]
                best_idx = idx
        arr, local_val = remaining.pop(best_idx)

        if arr.size <= 8192:
            try:
                exact_val = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
            except Exception:
                exact_val = local_val
        else:
            exact_val = local_val

        if np.isfinite(exact_val) and exact_val < final_exact_best:
            final_exact_best = exact_val
            final_arr = arr.copy()
            print(f"exact candidate: n={arr.size}, exact={exact_val:.8f}")

    if final_arr is None:
        if scored_candidates:
            # Pick minimum local value without lambda.
            best_arr = tracked_arr = None
            best_val = np.inf
            for arr, val in scored_candidates:
                if val < best_val:
                    best_val = val
                    tracked_arr = arr
            final_arr = tracked_arr
            final_exact_best = best_val
        else:
            final_arr = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
            final_exact_best = _score_from_c(_conv_self(final_arr), final_arr.size)

    # If substantial time remains, one more tiny round of pair polish on exact best.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if (time_left > 80.0 and final_arr is not None and final_arr.size <= 8192):
        trial_budget = min(time_left - 60.0, 120.0)
        if trial_budget > 20.0:
            print(f"final extra pair polish for {trial_budget:.0f}s")
            a_extra, o_extra = _pair_polish(final_arr, trial_budget, seed=int(seed) + 91, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(o_extra):
                try:
                    exact_extra = evaluate_sequence(a_extra.tolist())
                except Exception:
                    exact_extra = o_extra
                if np.isfinite(exact_extra) and exact_extra < final_exact_best:
                    final_arr = a_extra
                    final_exact_best = exact_extra

    final = np.asarray(final_arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(final.sum())
    final = final / s

    # Final exact validation and fallback.
    try:
        last_exact = evaluate_sequence(final.tolist())
    except Exception:
        last_exact = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(last_exact):
        if given_a is not None:
            final = _normalize(given_a, 0.0)
        else:
            final = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        print("final fallback to given/ones")

    try:
        last_exact2 = evaluate_sequence(final.tolist())
    except Exception:
        last_exact2 = np.inf

    print(f"FINAL returned: n={final.size}, local_best={global_best:.8f}, exact={float(last_exact2):.8f}")

    return final.astype(float).tolist()
```
